[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +        HAL_NVIC_DisableIRQ(SPI6_IRQn);
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +        NVIC_ClearPendingIRQ(SPI6_IRQn);
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +/* ==========================================================================================
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| + * Phase F3: spi_hal on CPU1 (plan revision 3: T1, T2 (with the raw HAL image, R3-4), T4

[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +/* ---- The test's own SpiBm_Config_t -> SPI_InitTypeDef, for the raw-HAL image ----------- */

[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +static const uint32_t k_hal_brp[8] = {
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    SPI_BAUDRATEPRESCALER_2, SPI_BAUDRATEPRESCALER_4, SPI_BAUDRATEPRESCALER_8, SPI_BAUDRATEPRESCALER_16,
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    SPI_BAUDRATEPRESCALER_32, SPI_BAUDRATEPRESCALER_64, SPI_BAUDRATEPRESCALER_128, SPI_BAUDRATEPRESCALER_256 };

[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    SPI_MASTER_SS_IDLENESS_06CYCLE, SPI_MASTER_SS_IDLENESS_07CYCLE, SPI_MASTER_SS_IDLENESS_08CYCLE,
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    SPI_MASTER_SS_IDLENESS_09CYCLE, SPI_MASTER_SS_IDLENESS_10CYCLE, SPI_MASTER_SS_IDLENESS_11CYCLE,
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    SPI_MASTER_SS_IDLENESS_12CYCLE, SPI_MASTER_SS_IDLENESS_13CYCLE, SPI_MASTER_SS_IDLENESS_14CYCLE,
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    SPI_MASTER_SS_IDLENESS_15CYCLE };

[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +static void sst_hal_of(const SpiBm_Config_t *b, SPI_InitTypeDef *in)
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->Mode                       = SPI_MODE_MASTER;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->Direction                  = (b->direction == SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY) ? SPI_DIRECTION_2LINES_TXONLY : SPI_DIRECTION_2LINES;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->DataSize                   = k_hal_size[(b->data_bits - 4U) % 29U];
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->CLKPolarity                = ((b->mode & 2U) != 0U) ? SPI_POLARITY_HIGH : SPI_POLARITY_LOW;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->CLKPhase                   = ((b->mode & 1U) != 0U) ? SPI_PHASE_2EDGE : SPI_PHASE_1EDGE;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->NSS                        = (b->nss == SPI_BM_NSS_SOFT) ? SPI_NSS_SOFT : SPI_NSS_HARD_OUTPUT;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->BaudRatePrescaler          = k_hal_brp[b->mbr & 7U];
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->FirstBit                   = b->lsb_first ? SPI_FIRSTBIT_LSB : SPI_FIRSTBIT_MSB;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->TIMode                     = SPI_TIMODE_DISABLE;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->CRCCalculation             = SPI_CRCCALCULATION_DISABLE;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->CRCPolynomial              = 0x7U;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->CRCLength                  = SPI_CRC_LENGTH_DATASIZE;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->NSSPMode                   = (b->nss == SPI_BM_NSS_HW_PULSE) ? SPI_NSS_PULSE_ENABLE : SPI_NSS_PULSE_DISABLE;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->NSSPolarity                = b->nss_active_high ? SPI_NSS_POLARITY_HIGH : SPI_NSS_POLARITY_LOW;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->FifoThreshold              = k_hal_fifo[(b->fifo_threshold - 1U) & 15U];
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->TxCRCInitializationPattern = SPI_CRC_INITIALIZATION_ALL_ZERO_PATTERN;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->RxCRCInitializationPattern = SPI_CRC_INITIALIZATION_ALL_ZERO_PATTERN;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->MasterSSIdleness           = k_hal_mssi[b->mssi & 15U];
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->MasterInterDataIdleness    = k_hal_midi[b->midi & 15U];
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->MasterReceiverAutoSusp     = SPI_MASTER_RX_AUTOSUSP_DISABLE;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->MasterKeepIOState          = b->keep_pins ? SPI_MASTER_KEEP_IO_STATE_ENABLE : SPI_MASTER_KEEP_IO_STATE_DISABLE;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    in->IOSwap                     = SPI_IO_SWAP_DISABLE;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T1 with spi_hal (CPU1: SPI2, SPI4, SPI6) ------------------------------------------ */
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +static DMA_HandleTypeDef s_hd_dummy;     /* a linked, never started DMA handle (T1 only) */
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +static void f3_t1(void)
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *const inst[4] = { SPI2, SPI4, SPI5, SPI6 };
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 4U; i++)
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_BM_EnableClock(inst[i]);
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(inst[i]);
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t a[T1_SNAP];
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t b[T1_SNAP];
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t same = 0U;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(SPI2, 7U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    SpiBm_Config_t k;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    SPI_HandleTypeDef *h = &s_h2;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    s_t1_n = 0U;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    /* 1. Nothing initialised: the handle, the instance, the configuration, the calls. */
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(a);
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    h->Instance = SPI2;
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Init(NULL, &c), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Init(h, NULL), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.646s] [host] diff| +    h->Instance = (SPI_TypeDef *)USART3;                      t1(SPI_HAL_Init(h, &c), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->Instance = SPI4;                                       t1(SPI_HAL_Init(h, &c), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);  /* != cfg->spi */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->Instance = SPI2;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.data_bits = 3U;                                  t1(SPI_HAL_Init(h, &k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.mode = 4U;                                       t1(SPI_HAL_Init(h, &k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.mssi = 1U;                                       t1(SPI_HAL_Init(h, &k), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.data_bits = 32U; k.fifo_threshold = 3U;          t1(SPI_HAL_Init(h, &k), SPI_BM_ERR_RANGE);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    s_h6.Instance = SPI6;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.spi = SPI6; k.data_bits = 17U;                   t1(SPI_HAL_Init(&s_h6, &k), SPI_BM_ERR_UNSUPPORTED);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    s_h4.Instance = SPI4;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.spi = SPI4; k.data_bits = 16U; k.fifo_threshold = 3U;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +                                                              t1(SPI_HAL_Init(&s_h4, &k), SPI_BM_ERR_RANGE);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Deinit(NULL), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->Instance = (SPI_TypeDef *)USART3;                      t1(SPI_HAL_Deinit(h), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->Instance = SPI2;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Wait(NULL, 10U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Wait(h, 0U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Transfer(NULL, s_tx, s_rx, 1U, 10U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_StartTransferIt(NULL, s_tx, s_rx, 1U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->hdmatx = NULL;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->hdmarx = NULL;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_StartTransferDma(h, s_tx, NULL, 1U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);                     /* no hdmatx */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(b);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    same |= (memcmp(a, b, sizeof(a)) == 0) ? 1U : 0U;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    /* 2. SPI2 transmit only through spi_hal, SPI5 16-bit: the transfer's parameters. */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.direction = SPI_BM_DIR_TX_ONLY;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t i2 = SPI_HAL_Init(h, &k);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    s_h5.Instance = SPI5;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.spi = SPI5; k.data_bits = 16U;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t i5 = SPI_HAL_Init(&s_h5, &k);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(a);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Transfer(h, s_tx, NULL, 1U, 0U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);                         /* timeout 0 */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Transfer(h, s_tx, NULL, 0U, 10U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Transfer(h, s_tx, NULL, 65536U, 10U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Transfer(h, NULL, NULL, 1U, 10U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Transfer(h, s_tx, s_rx, 1U, 10U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);                        /* rx in TX_ONLY */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Transfer(&s_h5, (const uint8_t *)s_tx + 1U, NULL, 1U, 10U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);   /* odd */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_StartTransferIt(h, s_tx, NULL, 0U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->hdmatx = &s_hd_dummy;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_StartTransferDma(h, s_tx, s_rx, 1U), SPI_BM_ERR_PARAM);                     /* rx, no hdmarx */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(b);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    same |= (memcmp(a, b, sizeof(a)) == 0) ? 2U : 0U;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_HAL_Deinit(&s_h5);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    /* 3. SPE = 1 by hand, and the handle busy: refused before any HAL call. */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    k = c;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t i3 = SPI_HAL_Init(h, &k);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    SPI2->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t spe = SPI2->CR1 & SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(a);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Init(h, &k), SPI_BM_ERR_BUSY);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Transfer(h, s_tx, s_rx, 1U, 10U), SPI_BM_ERR_BUSY);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_StartTransferIt(h, s_tx, s_rx, 1U), SPI_BM_ERR_BUSY);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->hdmarx = &s_hd_dummy;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_StartTransferDma(h, s_tx, s_rx, 1U), SPI_BM_ERR_BUSY);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(b);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    same |= (memcmp(a, b, sizeof(a)) == 0) ? 4U : 0U;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    SPI2->CR1 &= ~SPI_CR1_SPE;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->State = HAL_SPI_STATE_BUSY_TX_RX;                      /* the handle says busy, the SPI is at rest */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1(SPI_HAL_Transfer(h, s_tx, s_rx, 1U, 10U), SPI_BM_ERR_BUSY);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->State = HAL_SPI_STATE_READY;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->hdmatx = NULL;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->hdmarx = NULL;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    /* 4. AFCNTR = 1 in force: another CPOL or SSIOP. */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    k = c; k.keep_pins = true;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t i4 = SPI_HAL_Init(h, &k);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(a);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    k.mode = 2U;                                     t1(SPI_HAL_Init(h, &k), SPI_BM_ERR_BUSY);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    k.mode = 0U; k.nss_active_high = true;           t1(SPI_HAL_Init(h, &k), SPI_BM_ERR_BUSY);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(b);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    same |= (memcmp(a, b, sizeof(a)) == 0) ? 8U : 0U;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t d4 = SPI_HAL_Deinit(h);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const bool reset_state = (h->State == HAL_SPI_STATE_RESET) && (reset_diff(SPI2) == 0U);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    /* The control: a valid SPI_HAL_Init changes the snapshot. */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(a);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    k = c;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t w = SPI_HAL_Init(h, &k);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    t1_snap(b);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const bool ctrl = memcmp(a, b, sizeof(a)) != 0;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_HAL_Deinit(h);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; (i < s_t1_n) && (i < T1_MAX); i++)
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +        if (s_t1_got[i] != s_t1_exp[i])
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +            bad++;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T1 HAL CM7 case %lu: status %u, expected %u", (unsigned long)i, (unsigned)s_t1_got[i],
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned)s_t1_exp[i]);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    verdict((bad == 0U) && (s_t1_n <= T1_MAX) && (same == 0xFU) && (spe != 0U) && (i2 == SPI_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +            (i5 == SPI_BM_OK) && (i3 == SPI_BM_OK) && (i4 == SPI_BM_OK),
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +            "T1 HAL CM7 refusals through spi_hal on SPI2, SPI4, SPI5, SPI6: %lu cases, %lu with another status; "
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +            "registers of the four and RCC_C1/C2 APB1LENR/APB2ENR/APB4ENR unchanged in the 4 groups: 0x%lX (0xF); "
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +            "SPE set by hand %u", (unsigned long)s_t1_n, (unsigned long)bad, (unsigned long)same, (unsigned)(spe != 0U));
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    verdict(ctrl && (w == SPI_BM_OK), "T1 HAL CM7 control: a valid SPI_HAL_Init(SPI2) changes the snapshot (%u)",
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)ctrl);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    verdict((d4 == SPI_BM_OK) && reset_state, "T14 HAL SPI_HAL_Deinit(SPI2) after a keep_pins Init: status %u, "
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +            "handle in RESET and registers at the reset image %u", (unsigned)d4, (unsigned)reset_state);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T2 with spi_hal: the image of SPI_HAL_Init against the raw HAL_SPI_Init (bit for
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +   bit), against spi_bm (bit for bit but for the differences by design) and against the
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +   RM table. The one difference by design found in the HAL source: CR1.SSI with hardware
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +   SS (stm32h7xx_hal_spi.c l. 375-381 set SSI only for a soft SS active low). ---------- */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +typedef struct { uint32_t n, bad_raw, bad_bm, design, bad_rm, bad_st, first_i, first_reg, got, exp; } T2HalCount_t;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +static void t2hal_one(const SpiBm_Config_t *c, T2HalCount_t *t)
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *spi = c->spi;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    SPI_HandleTypeDef *h = hal_h(spi);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t pb[NREG];
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t ph[NREG];
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t pr[NREG];
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t sb = SPI_BM_Init(c);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    spi_regs(spi, pb);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->Instance = spi;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t sh = SPI_HAL_Init(h, c);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    spi_regs(spi, ph);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t dh = SPI_HAL_Deinit(h);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    /* the raw HAL: the reset image, then HAL_SPI_Init with no wrapper */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    h->State = HAL_SPI_STATE_RESET;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    sst_hal_of(c, &h->Init);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const HAL_StatusTypeDef hr = HAL_SPI_Init(h);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    spi_regs(spi, pr);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_HAL_Deinit(h);
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    if ((sb != SPI_BM_OK) || (sh != SPI_BM_OK) || (dh != SPI_BM_OK) || (hr != HAL_OK))
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +        t->bad_st++;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    if (memcmp(ph, pr, sizeof(ph)) != 0)
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +        t->bad_raw++;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    /* spi_bm writes CR1.SSI inactive with any SS; the HAL only with a soft SS */
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t design = ((c->nss != SPI_BM_NSS_SOFT) && !c->nss_active_high) ? SPI_CR1_SSI : 0U;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    if (((ph[0] ^ pb[0]) == design) && (design != 0U))
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +        t->design++;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t r = 0U; r < NREG; r++)
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t allowed = (r == 0U) ? design : 0U;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +        if ((ph[r] ^ pb[r]) != allowed)
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +            if (t->bad_bm == 0U)
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +                t->first_i = t->n;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +                t->first_reg = r;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +                t->got = ph[r];
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +                t->exp = pb[r];
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +            t->bad_bm++;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +            break;
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.647s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t e1 = 0U;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t e2 = 0U;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t ecr = 0U;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    sst_t2_expect(c, &e1, &e2, &ecr);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    if ((ph[2] != e1) || (ph[3] != e2) || (ph[0] != (ecr ^ design)))
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +        t->bad_rm++;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    t->n++;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +static void t2hal_inst(SPI_TypeDef *spi, const char *name)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    SpiBm_Caps_t caps = { 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_GetCaps(spi, &caps);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t bits_list[4] = { 4U, 8U, 16U, 32U };
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t nb = (caps.max_data_bits == 32U) ? 4U : 3U;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    T2HalCount_t t = { 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_EnableClock(spi);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_BM_Deinit(spi);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t mbr = 0U; mbr <= 7U; mbr++)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t mode = 0U; mode <= 3U; mode++)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +            for (uint32_t ib = 0U; ib < nb; ib++)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                const uint32_t bits = bits_list[ib];
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                const uint32_t thr[2] = { 1U, thr_max(spi, bits) };
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                for (uint32_t it = 0U; it < 2U; it++)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                    for (uint32_t lsb = 0U; lsb < 2U; lsb++)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                    {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                        for (uint32_t dir = 0U; dir < 2U; dir++)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                        {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                            for (uint32_t nss = 0U; nss < 3U; nss++)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                            {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                for (uint32_t op = 0U; op < 2U; op++)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                    for (uint32_t mi = 0U; mi < 4U; mi++)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                    {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                        const uint32_t mssi = ((mi & 1U) != 0U) ? 15U : 0U;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                        const uint32_t midi = ((mi & 2U) != 0U) ? 15U : 0U;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                        if ((nss == SPI_BM_NSS_SOFT) && (mssi != 0U))
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                        {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                            continue;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                        }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                        for (uint32_t keep = 0U; keep < 2U; keep++)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                        {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                            const SpiBm_Config_t c = { spi, mbr, mode, bits, lsb != 0U, dir, thr[it],
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                                                       nss, op != 0U, mssi, midi, keep != 0U };
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                            t2hal_one(&c, &t);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                        }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                    }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                            }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                        }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                    }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    /* The control: one bit flipped in the spi_hal photo (MASTER in CFG2) is caught by the
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +       same comparison with the raw HAL image. */
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c0 = mkcfg(spi, 0U, 0U, 8U, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    SPI_HandleTypeDef *h = hal_h(spi);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t ph[NREG];
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t pr[NREG];
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    h->Instance = spi;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_HAL_Init(h, &c0);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    spi_regs(spi, ph);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_HAL_Deinit(h);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    h->State = HAL_SPI_STATE_RESET;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    sst_hal_of(&c0, &h->Init);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    (void)HAL_SPI_Init(h);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    spi_regs(spi, pr);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    (void)SPI_HAL_Deinit(h);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    ph[3] ^= SPI_CFG2_MASTER;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    const bool caught = memcmp(ph, pr, sizeof(ph)) != 0;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    if (t.bad_bm != 0U)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +        LOG("T2 HAL %s first difference from spi_bm outside the design: config %lu register %lu HAL 0x%08lX BM 0x%08lX",
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +            name, (unsigned long)t.first_i, (unsigned long)t.first_reg, (unsigned long)t.got, (unsigned long)t.exp);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    verdict((t.bad_raw == 0U) && (t.bad_bm == 0U) && (t.bad_rm == 0U) && (t.bad_st == 0U) && caught,
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +            "T2 HAL %s %lu configurations: SPI_HAL_Init image differs from the raw HAL_SPI_Init image in %lu, from "
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +            "spi_bm's outside the differences by design in %lu (CR1.SSI with hardware SS, active low: %lu "
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +            "configurations), CFG1/CFG2/CR1 from the RM0399 table in %lu; a status other than OK %lu; control (MASTER "
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +            "flipped in the spi_hal photo) caught %u", name, (unsigned long)t.n, (unsigned long)t.bad_raw,
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)t.bad_bm, (unsigned long)t.design, (unsigned long)t.bad_rm, (unsigned long)t.bad_st,
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)caught);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T7(b) with spi_hal: SPI6 by BDMA (dma_hal), the HAL's DMA path ------------------- */
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +static SpiBm_Status_t t7b_one_hal(uint32_t bits, uint32_t n, uint32_t seed, bool rx, uint32_t *mism,
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                                  uint32_t *left)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t es = sst_elem_bytes(bits);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t mbr = 2U;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Config_t c = mkcfg(SPI6, mbr, 0U, bits, false, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill(s_bd_tx, es, n, bits, seed);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    sst_fill_const(s_bd_rx, es, n, 0xA5A5A5A5UL);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    s_h6.Instance = SPI6;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t si = SPI_HAL_Init(&s_h6, &c);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    const bool d_ok = bd_init_irq(&s_hbd_rx, BDMA_Channel0, BDMA_REQUEST_SPI6_RX, DMA_PERIPH_TO_MEMORY, es) &&
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +                      bd_init_irq(&s_hbd_tx, BDMA_Channel1, BDMA_REQUEST_SPI6_TX, DMA_MEMORY_TO_PERIPH, es);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    __HAL_LINKDMA(&s_h6, hdmatx, s_hbd_tx);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    __HAL_LINKDMA(&s_h6, hdmarx, s_hbd_rx);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    nvic_spi6(true);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    s_hal_busy = true;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    SpiBm_Status_t st = SPI_HAL_StartTransferDma(&s_h6, s_bd_tx, rx ? s_bd_rx : NULL, n);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    if (st != SPI_BM_OK)
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +        s_hal_busy = false;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +        st = SPI_HAL_Wait(&s_h6, ms_of(budget_of(SPI6, mbr, bits, n)));
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    s_hal_busy = false;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    nvic_spi6(false);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    (void)DMA_HAL_DeInit(&s_hbd_tx);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    (void)DMA_HAL_DeInit(&s_hbd_rx);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    s_h6.hdmatx = NULL;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    s_h6.hdmarx = NULL;
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t sd = SPI_HAL_Deinit(&s_h6);
[+   1.648s] [host] diff| +    *mism = sst_mismatch(s_bd_tx, s_bd_rx, es, n, bits);
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t l = 0U;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < n; i++)
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        if (sst_get(s_bd_rx, es, i) == (0xA5A5A5A5UL & sst_mask(es * 8U)))
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +            l++;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    *left = l;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    if ((si != SPI_BM_OK) || !d_ok || (sd != SPI_BM_OK))
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        return SPI_BM_ERR_HAL;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    return st;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +static void t7b_hal(void)
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < 2U; b++)
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t bits = (b == 0U) ? 8U : 16U;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t frames[4] = { 1U, 9U, 255U, BD_MAXF };
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < 4U; k++)
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t seed = next_seed();
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +            uint32_t mism = 0U;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +            uint32_t left = 0U;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +            const SpiBm_Status_t st = t7b_one_hal(bits, frames[k], seed, true, &mism, &left);
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t crc = sst_crc32_frames(s_bd_rx, sst_elem_bytes(bits), frames[k], bits);
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T7B SPI6/HAL b%lu n%lu s=%08lX st=%u mis=%lu crc=%08lX", (unsigned long)bits,
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)frames[k], (unsigned long)seed, (unsigned)st, (unsigned long)mism, (unsigned long)crc);
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +            if ((st != SPI_BM_OK) || (mism != 0U))
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +                bad++;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t mism = 0U;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t left = 0U;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    const SpiBm_Status_t st = t7b_one_hal(8U, 255U, next_seed(), false, &mism, &left);
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad == 0U, "T7(b) SPI6/HAL by BDMA (HAL_SPI_TransmitReceive_DMA, channels 0/1, buffers in .d3_ram), "
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +            "8/16 bits, 1..8192 frames: %lu failed", (unsigned long)bad);
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    verdict((st == SPI_BM_OK) && (left == 255U), "T7(b) SPI6/HAL control, no RX stream (HAL_SPI_Transmit_DMA, "
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +            "COMM = transmit only), 255 frames: status %u, RX words with the sentinel %lu (255)", (unsigned)st,
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)left);
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +/* ---- The F3 steps ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +static void step_f3_nopin(void)
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    if (!step_begin("F3_NOPIN"))
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    tick_line("F3_NOPIN start");
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    f3_t1();
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    t2hal_inst(SPI2, "SPI2");
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    tick_line("F3_NOPIN T2 SPI2");
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    t2hal_inst(SPI3, "SPI3");
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    t2hal_inst(SPI4, "SPI4");
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    t2hal_inst(SPI5, "SPI5");
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    t2hal_inst(SPI6, "SPI6");
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    SPI_TypeDef *const inst[5] = { SPI2, SPI3, SPI4, SPI5, SPI6 };
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    const char *const names[5] = { "SPI2", "SPI3", "SPI4", "SPI5", "SPI6" };
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 5U; i++)
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Config_t cb = mkcfg(inst[i], 3U, 1U, 8U, true, SPI_BM_DIR_FULL_DUPLEX, 1U);
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        SPI_HandleTypeDef *h = hal_h(inst[i]);
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        h->Instance = inst[i];
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        (void)SPI_HAL_Init(h, &cb);
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        const SpiBm_Status_t sd = SPI_HAL_Deinit(h);
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t d = reset_diff(inst[i]);
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        verdict((sd == SPI_BM_OK) && (d == 0U) && (h->State == HAL_SPI_STATE_RESET),
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +                "T14 HAL %s SPI_HAL_Deinit after SPI_HAL_Init: %u, registers off the reset image 0x%02lX (0), handle "
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +                "RESET %u", names[i], (unsigned)sd, (unsigned long)d, (unsigned)(h->State == HAL_SPI_STATE_RESET));
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    tick_line("F3_NOPIN end");
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    if (!sync0())
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +    step_end();
[+   1.649s] [host] diff| +}
